00499: /* =========================
00500:    CONCURRENCY
00501: ========================= */
00502: 
00503: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00504:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00505:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00506:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00507:   let idx = 0;
00508: 
00509:   async function worker() {
00510:     while (true) {
00511:       const current = idx;
00512:       idx += 1;
00513:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00514:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00515:     }
00516:   }
00517: 
00518:   await Promise.all(
00519:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00520:   );
00521:   return results;
00522: }

00524: /* =========================
00525:    ROUTE
00526: ========================= */
00527: 
00528: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00529:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00530:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00531:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00532: 
00533:   let status = "empty";
00534:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00535:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00537:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00538:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";

00387:   }
00388:   return null;

00503: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00504:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00505:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00506:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00507:   let idx = 0;
00508: 
00509:   async function worker() {

00511:       const current = idx;
00512:       idx += 1;
00513:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00514:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00515:     }
00516:   }
00517: 
00518:   await Promise.all(
00519:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00520:   );
00521:   return results;
00522: }

00549: function buildSlots(opts) {
00550:   const {
00551:     scheduleAll,
00552:     byHour,
00553:     baseSoft,
00554:     removedHardApplied,
00555:     removedSoftApplied,
00556:     expectedHard,
00557:     expectedSoft,
00558:     expectedHardPublished,
00559:     expectedSoftPublished,
00560:     capToday,
00561:     isToday,
00562:     nowMinBR,
00563:     slotGraceMin,
00564:   } = opts || {};
00565: 
00566:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00567:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00568: 
00569:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00570:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00571: 
00572:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00573:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00574:     }
00575: 
00576:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00577: 
00578:     if (
00579:       capToday &&
00580:       isToday &&
00581:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00582:     ) {
00583:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00584:       if (!published) {
00585:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00586:       }
00587:     }
00588: 
00589:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00590:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00591:     }
00592:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00593:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00594:     }
00595: 
00596:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00597:   });
00598: }

00503: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00504:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00505:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00506:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00507:   let idx = 0;
00508: 
00509:   async function worker() {
00510:     while (true) {
00511:       const current = idx;
00512:       idx += 1;
00513:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00514:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00515:     }
00516:   }
00517: 
00518:   await Promise.all(
00519:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00520:   );
00521:   return results;
00522: }

00530:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00531:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00532:

00534:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00535:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00537:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00538:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00539: 
00540:   return {
00541:     status,
00542:     expected,
00543:     found,
00544:     missing,
00545:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00546:   };

00549: function buildSlots(opts) {
00550:   const {
00551:     scheduleAll,
00552:     byHour,
00553:     baseSoft,
00554:     removedHardApplied,
00555:     removedSoftApplied,
00556:     expectedHard,
00557:     expectedSoft,
00558:     expectedHardPublished,
00559:     expectedSoftPublished,
00560:     capToday,
00561:     isToday,
00562:     nowMinBR,
00563:     slotGraceMin,
00564:   } = opts || {};
00565: 
00566:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00567:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00568: 
00569:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00570:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00571: 
00572:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00573:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00574:     }
00575: 
00576:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00577: 
00578:     if (
00579:       capToday &&
00580:       isToday &&
00581:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00582:     ) {
00583:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00584:       if (!published) {
00585:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00586:       }
00587:     }
00588: 
00589:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00590:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00591:     }
00592:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00593:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00594:     }
00595: 
00596:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };

00491: function getGapsForDate(lotteryKey, ymd, opts) {

00499: /* =========================
00500:    CONCURRENCY
00501: ========================= */
00502: 
00503: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00504:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00505:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00506:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00507:   let idx = 0;
00508: 
00509:   async function worker() {
00510:     while (true) {
00511:       const current = idx;
00512:       idx += 1;
00513:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00514:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00515:     }
00516:   }
00517: 
00518:   await Promise.all(
00519:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00520:   );
00521:   return results;
00522: }

00528: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00529:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00530:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00531:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00532: 
00533:   let status = "empty";
00534:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00535:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00537:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00538:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00539: 
00540:   return {
00541:     status,
00542:     expected,
00543:     found,
00544:     missing,
00545:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00546:   };
00547: }

00549: function buildSlots(opts) {
00550:   const {
00551:     scheduleAll,
00552:     byHour,
00553:     baseSoft,
00554:     removedHardApplied,
00555:     removedSoftApplied,
00556:     expectedHard,
00557:     expectedSoft,
00558:     expectedHardPublished,
00559:     expectedSoftPublished,
00560:     capToday,
00561:     isToday,
00562:     nowMinBR,
00563:     slotGraceMin,
00564:   } = opts || {};
00565: 
00566:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00567:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00568: 
00569:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00570:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00571: 
00572:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00573:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00574:     }
00575: 
00576:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00577: 
00578:     if (
00579:       capToday &&
00580:       isToday &&
00581:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00582:     ) {
00583:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00584:       if (!published) {
00585:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00586:       }
00587:     }
00588: 
00589:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00590:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00591:     }
00592:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00593:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00594:     }
00595: 
00596:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00597:   });
00598: }

00528: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00529:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00530:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00531:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00532: 
00533:   let status = "empty";
00534:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00535:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00537:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00538:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00539: 
00540:   return {
00541:     status,
00542:     expected,
00543:     found,
00544:     missing,
00545:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00546:   };
00547: }
00548: 
00549: function buildSlots(opts) {
00550:   const {
00551:     scheduleAll,
00552:     byHour,
00553:     baseSoft,
00554:     removedHardApplied,
00555:     removedSoftApplied,
00556:     expectedHard,
00557:     expectedSoft,
00558:     expectedHardPublished,
00559:     expectedSoftPublished,
00560:     capToday,
00561:     isToday,
00562:     nowMinBR,
00563:     slotGraceMin,
00564:   } = opts || {};
00565: 
00566:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00567:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00568: 
00569:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00570:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00571: 
00572:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00573:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00574:     }
00575: 
00576:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00577: 
00578:     if (
00579:       capToday &&
00580:       isToday &&
00581:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00582:     ) {
00583:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00584:       if (!published) {
00585:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00586:       }
00587:     }
00588: 
00589:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00590:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00591:     }
00592:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00593:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00594:     }
00595: 
00596:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00597:   });
00598: }

00846:           const a = String(prev.close_hour || "");
00847:           const b = String(dr.close_hour || "");
00848:           if (b && (!a || b < a)) byHour.set(hh, dr);
00849:         }

00929: 
00930:       const prizesSnap = await doc.ref.collection("prizes").orderBy("position", "asc").get();
00931:

00933:         id: doc.id,

00945:       const hh = hourFromCloseHourSlot(dr?.close_hour, lotteryKey);
00946:       if (!hh) continue;

00948:       if (!prev) {
00949:         byHour.set(hh, dr);
00950:       } else {
00951:         const a = String(prev.close_hour || "");
00952:         const b = String(dr.close_hour || "");
00953:         if (b && (!a || b < a)) byHour.set(hh, dr);
00954:       }
00955:     }

00941:     draws.sort((a, b) => cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour));
00942: 
00943:     const byHour = new Map();
00944:     for (const dr of draws) {
00945:       const hh = hourFromCloseHourSlot(dr?.close_hour, lotteryKey);
00946:       if (!hh) continue;
00947:       const prev = byHour.get(hh);
00948:       if (!prev) {
00949:         byHour.set(hh, dr);
00950:       } else {
00951:         const a = String(prev.close_hour || "");
00952:         const b = String(dr.close_hour || "");
00953:         if (b && (!a || b < a)) byHour.set(hh, dr);
00954:       }

00988:       foundTotal: presentHours.length,

00990:       removedSoft: removedSoftApplied.size,
00991:       missingHard: slots.filter((s) => s.status === "missing").length,
00992:       missingSoft: slots.filter((s) => s.status === "soft_missing").length,

00990:       removedSoft: removedSoftApplied.size,
00991:       missingHard: slots.filter((s) => s.status === "missing").length,
00992:       missingSoft: slots.filter((s) => s.status === "soft_missing").length,
00993:       missingTotal:
00994:         slots.filter((s) => s.status === "missing").length +
00995:         slots.filter((s) => s.status === "soft_missing").length,
00996:       completionPct:
00997:         expectedHard.length + expectedSoft.length
00998:           ? Number(
00999:               (
01000:                 (presentHours.length /
01001:                   (expectedHard.length + expectedSoft.length)) *
01002:                 100
01003:               ).toFixed(1)
01004:             )
01005:           : 100,
01006:     };
01007: 
01008:     return res.json({
01009:       ok: true,
01010:       date,
01011:       lottery,
01012:       strict,
01013:       includePrizes,
01014:       limitDocs,
01015:       docsFound,
01016:       docsCapped,
01017:       dayStatus,
01018:       blocked: false,
01019:       count: draws.length,
01020:       draws,
01021:       expectedHard,
01022:       expectedSoft,
01023:       presentHours,
01024:       slotsSummary,
01025:       calendarStatus: buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked: false, isToday }),
01026:       slots,
01027:     });
01028:   } catch (e) {
01029:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
01030:   }
01031: });
